Line search details:
```
def try_update(f, h, n, eta, mode):
  if mode mult:
    cat = eta * (h - np.mean(h))
    cat = np.clip(cat, -maxlog, maxlog)
    nf = f * np.exp(cat)
  else:
    nf = f - eta*(h - np.mean(h))
    nf = np.maximum(nf,0)
  s=nf.sum(); if s<... return inf
  nf *= n/s
  C=conv_scaled(nf); return C.max()*2
```

Pseudo code structure:

```python
""" docstring """
import numpy as np, time, math, sys

def next_pow2(m): ...
def conv_scaled(f, fft_size=None):
    n=len(f)
    if fft_size is None: fft_size = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, fft_size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, fft_size)
    return c.[:2*n-1]/n
def score_f(f): return 2*conv_scaled(f).max()
def eval_list(a): ... using evaluate_sequence
def norm_sum1(arr): ... valid floats
def norm_f(arr, n=None): arr=max0, n if None len, sum, if sum<... ones*n, return arr*n/sum
def top_weights(C, K, beta):
   m=len(C); K=min(K,m); idx=np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:] ; vals=C[idx]; maxv=vals.max(); if beta==0 weights=1/K else exp clip; normalize; sort maybe descending? no need.
   return idx, weights
def active_grad(f, idx, weights, n):
   g=np.zeros(n); scale=2.0*n  # shape gradient scale n*gradC
   for k,w in zip(idx,weights):
     if w < 1e-12: continue
     lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
     if lo>=hi: continue
     # i = lo..hi-1; j=k-i
     # vector j = k - np.arange(lo,hi)
     # g[lo:hi] += scale*w*f[j]
     g[lo:hi] += scale*w*f[k - np.arange(lo,hi)]
   return g
```
But `np.arange` inside loop heavy. Precompute arange? For n variable, can in helper create `idxs = np.arange(lo,hi)`; overhead okay. Could optimize with preallocated full index array? For each k, we can use slice of `revf`? Since j = k-i, as i increases j decreases. We can slice f segment from j_hi down to j_lo. For k fixed:
   i_lo=max(0,k-n+1), i_hi=min(n,k+1)
   j_hi = k - i_lo; j_lo = k - (i_hi-1)
   Then f_segment = f[j_lo:j_hi+1][::-1] (length i_hi-i_lo). g[i_lo:i_hi] += scale*w*f_segment. This avoids arange. Good.
```
seg = f[j_lo:j_hi+1]
g[i_lo:i_hi] += scale*w*seg[::-1]
```

def cand_score(f_new, fft_size): C=conv_scaled(f_new,fft_size); return 2*C.max()
def update_mult(f,h,eta,n,fft_size,maxlog=4):
   hc=h - h.mean()
   cat = eta*hc
   cat = np.clip(cat, -maxlog, maxlog)
   nf = f*np.exp(cat)
   s=nf.sum()
   if s<=0: return None,inf
   nf *= n/s
   # maybe floor min? no
   return nf, score
```

Let's define a single "shape step" function that given f, C, beta, method, steps returns best candidate and C.
```
def one_shape_step(f, C, beta, n, fft_size, steps, modes):
    # compute w
    w = softmax_weights(C,beta,n)
    h = dense_grad(f,w,n,fft_size)  # uses 2*
    # center later in update
    best_f=f; best_C=C; best_sc=2*C.max(); best_eta=None
    for mode in modes:
       for eta in steps:
          nf,nC = apply_update(f,h,eta,n,mode,fft_size)
          sc=2*nC.max()
          if sc<best_sc: store
    return best_f,best_C,best_sc,best_eta
```
But computing h per candidate? h same for beta. Good.

Restart logic:
```
def smooth_perturb(f, rng, scale):
   # random low-frequency noise with zero mean
   N = len(f)
   Flen = 1<<... maybe next pow2 N
   w=np.zeros(N//2+1, dtype=complex)
   # choose random coefficients with decay
   w[1:] = (rng.normal(size=N//2)+1j*rng.normal(size=N//2))*np.exp(-np.linspace(0,2,N//2))
   p = np.fft.irfft(w, N)[:N]
   p = p - mean; if std>0 p*=scale/std
   npert = f + p*np.std(f)*? 
   return norm_f(npmaximum)
```
Clipping. Or more simply: `pert = f * (1 + scale * smooth_noise)` with noise zero mean. Need ensure nonnegative. Use `scale` 0.1-0.5. Blend with best: f_new = norm_f((1-w)*best + w*pert). Also can use pure random param candidates as restart:

   - Cosine with random phase/freq

`lbfgs_objective(u, n, betaC, fft_size, state)`:
   f=softmax_f(u,n,fft_size?) no.
   C=conv_scaled(f,fft_size)
   # soft start: m=C.max(); if betaC==0: Csm=m? But betaC small.
   shifted = betaC*(C-m)
   # sumexp: shifted<=0; use np.exp; sum.
   Csm = m + (1/betaC)*np.log(exp_shift.sum())
   J = n*Csm  # value ~Hmax
   # weights w = exp_shift/sum
   grad_f = 2*weighted_conv_shape(f,w,n,fft_size) # where weighted_conv_shape returns ∑ w f_{k-i} (no 2)
   g_center = grad_f - mean(grad_f)
   zgrad = f * g_center  # note grad_f already 2∑? wait define weighted_conv_shape no 2, then grad_f=2*...; okay.
   return J,zgrad

`weighted_conv_shape(f,w,n,fft_size)` returns `conv` segment (no factor 2). We'll implement `weighted_conv_no2` using FFT as before without *2. Then objective grad_f=2*that.

umax = u.max(); e = np.exp(u-umax); s=e.sum(); f=n*e/s
```

Detailed helper functions:

```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

`conv_scaled(f, fft_size=None)`:
```
n=len(f)
if fft_size is None: fft_size=next_pow2(2*n-1)
F=np.fft.rfft(f,fft_size)
C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,fft_size)
return C[:2*n-1]/float(n)
```
Be careful with float(n) to divide array.

`weighted_conv_no2(f,w,n,fft_size=None)`:
```
L=len(w)
if fft_size is None: fft_size=next_pow2(n+L-1)
F=np.fft.rfft(w,fft_size)
G=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],fft_size)
conv=np.fft.irfft(F*G,fft_size)
return conv[n-1:n+n]
```
For `f[::-1]` copy each call. Could avoid by computing FFT of reversed from FFT_f? But rfft of reverse is conjugate? For real, FFT(reverse(f)) = conj(FFT(f)) * phase? Specifically DFT of x[N-1-n] = conj(DFT(x)) * exp? If same length N. But simpler copy.

`softmax_weights_from_C(C,betaC, eps=1e-6)`:
```
m=C.max()
sh=betaC*(C-m)
sh=np.clip(sh, -700, 0)
e=np.exp(sh); s=e.sum()
if s<1e-300:
   # fallback top indices
   return np.ones(len(C))/len(C), m
w=e/s
if eps>0:
   w = (1-eps)*w + eps/len(C)
return w, m
```
If betaC=0, uniform.

`manual_step(f,C,betaC,n,fft_size,steps,modes,maxlog=5)`:
```
w,_=softmax_weights_from_C(C,betaC)
h=weighted_conv_no2(f,w,n,fft_size)
grad=2.0*h
centered = grad - grad.mean()
# maybe if std(centered) < 1e-12: return None
best_f=f; best_C=C; best_sc=2*C.max()
best_eta=None; best_mode=None
for mode in modes:
   for eta in steps:
      if mode==0: # mult
         cat=-eta*centered
         if maxlog: cat=np.clip(cat, -maxlog, maxlog)
         nf=f*np.exp(cat)
      else:
         nf=f - eta*centered
         # To prevent negative, use maximum; if all clipped maybe still sum
         if np.any(nf<0): nf=np.maximum(nf,0.0)
      s=nf.sum()
      if s<=1e-15: continue
      nf *= n/s
      Cn=conv_scaled(nf,fft_size)
      scn=2*Cn.max()
      if scn < best_sc:
         best_sc=scn; best_f=nf.copy(); best_C=Cn.copy(); best_eta=eta; best_mode=mode
return best_f,best_C,best_sc,best_eta,best_mode,centered
```
We can return Cn for accepted to update active. `best_C.copy()` maybe memory; if no candidate accept, original C. For candidate, if best, no need keep all. Good.
Potential speed: Precompute `cat_base = centered` and for each eta, `np.exp(-eta*centered)`; okay.
We can skip mode if eta too large? no.

`minimax_search`:
```
def minimax_search(f0, time_limit, seed=0, beta0=1.0, beta1=100.0, steps=None, verbose=True):
   t0=time.monotonic()
   f=norm_f(f0)
   n=len(f); fft_size=next_pow2(3*n-2) # also enough for b
   C=conv_scaled(f,fft_size); cur=2*C.max()
   best_f=f.copy(); best_sc=cur
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+12345)
   iter=0; stall=0; beta=beta0; cur_eta? not needed; last_mode=0
   steps = [0.02,0.06,0.18,0.55,1.6,4.0] if steps is None else steps
   modes = [0,1]
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
      if n==0 break
      # adaptive beta based on elapsed
      prog = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(time_limit,1e-6))
      beta = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*(1.0-math.exp(-3.0*prog))
      # if stall high, randomize beta upwards
      if stall > 30:
         beta = min(300.0, beta*1.2)
      bf,bC,bsc,eta,mode,centered = manual_step(f,C,beta,n,fft_size,steps,modes)
      if bsc < cur - 1e-13:
          f=bf; C=bC; cur=bsc
          if cur < best_sc:
             best_sc=cur; best_f=f.copy()
             if verbose: print(...)
          stall=0
      else:
          stall += 1
          if stall == 1 or stall % 25 == 0:
              # perturb around best
              f=smooth_restart(f,best_f,rng,best_sc,n)
              C=conv_scaled(f,fft_size); cur=2*C.max()
              if cur<best_sc: update
          if stall > 200:
              # more aggressive random start from parametric + best blend
              f=parametric_blend(best_f,n,rng,0.5)
              C=conv_scaled(f,fft_size); cur=2*C.max()
              stall=100
      iter +=1
      if verbose and iter%500==0: print progress
   return best_f,best_sc
```
Need `smooth_restart` top-level:
```
def smooth_restart(current, best, rng, scale, n):
   # If current too far, blend best
   base = norm_f((1-0.3)*best + 0.3*current)
   # generate smooth noise
   p = smooth_noise(n,rng,low_freq=20)
   pert = base + scale*p*np.std(base)
   return norm_f(pert)
```
`smooth_noise(n,rng,max_modes)`:
   Use FFT: length next_pow2(n); random coefficients for modes up to max; real inverse; zero mean.
```
def smooth_noise(n,rng,max_modes=30):
   N=next_pow2(n)
   F=np.zeros(N//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
   k=np.arange(1,min(max_modes,N//2)+1)
   amp=np.exp(-k/(max_modes/3.0))
   F[k]=(rng.normal(size=len(k))+1j*rng.normal(size=len(k)))*amp
   # conjugates not needed irfft
   vals=np.fft.irfft(F,N)[:n]
   vals-=vals.mean()
   sd=vals.std()
   if sd>0: vals/=sd
   return vals
```
Clipping: perturb may negative; norm_f clips.

`parametric_blend(best,n,rng,w)`:
   Generate candidate: uniform + smooth noise, sine, etc.
Maybe call `random_template(n,rng)`:
   x = arange/n; choose template:
      base = np.ones(n)/n or f=n*? We work f sum n.
   Templates f (sum n):
      uniform f ones.
      1 + a * smooth_noise
      1 + a1 sin(2π x + phase) + a2 cos(2πm x)
      exponential/taper: exp(α x) / mean; clipped.
   Normalize to f with n sum. Then blend w with best.

We need generate initial if no parent. Use a few random templates and choose best after quick minimax? Could simply parent. If no parent, set n=5200, generate a template with nonuniform and run. But parent likely exists.

Parameter `steps`: Need not too many. Let's choose `[0.02, 0.08, 0.25, 0.8, 2.5]` for both. 5 candidates*2 modes=10 per iter. If h small, perhaps no improvement. Could reduce to 3 modes? We'll optimize by first trying a single mid step (e.g. 0.25 or previous) and if improve accept, else full line search. Let's implement in `minimax_search`:
- `cur_eta = 0.25`, `cur_mode=0`.
- First candidate: apply mode/eta. If improved, accept and maybe adjust eta up/down; continue. If not, do line search over steps to find any candidate.

`lbfgs_objective`:
```
def lbfgs_objective(u, n, betaC, fft_size, state=None):
    # u 1d
    u = np.clip(u, -40.0, 40.0)  # for stability even if bounds
    e = np.exp(u - u.max())
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0: ...
    f = (n/s)*e
    C = conv_scaled(f, fft_size)
    m = C.max()
    sh = betaC*(C - m)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -700, 0)
    E = np.exp(sh); Es=E.sum()
    if Es <= 0: Csm=m; w=None
    else: Csm=m + np.log(Es)/betaC if betaC>1e-12 else m?; w=E/Es
    J = n * Csm
    if state is not None: state.last_J=J
    # weights
    if betaC < 1e-12: w = np.ones(len(C))/len(C)
    else: w=E/Es
    h = weighted_conv_no2(f,w,n,fft_size)
    grad_f = 2.0*h
    mean_g = np.dot(f, grad_f)/n? Since p=f/n, mean=∑p g = (dot(f,g)/n)
    # zgrad = f*(g - mean_g)
    zgrad = f * (grad_f - mean_g)
    return J, zgrad
```
Gradient derivation: f=n p, mean_g=∑p g. Yes dot(f,g)/n. Could use `g.mean()` if f sum n? Since g.mean() = ∑g/n, not weighted by p. Need weighted mean. Use `mean_g = np.sum(grad_f * f) / n`. Good.
At uniform f=1, grad_f constant? grad_f=2∑w f=2 constant (since w sum1), mean_g=2, zgrad zero. Good.
If f nonuniform, zgrad.

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    scipy_minimize = None

def next_pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def norm_f(arr):
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0:
       a=np.ones(0) # but need not
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    n=a.size
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(n)/s)
```
If arr empty? propose handles.

`conv_scaled`:
```
def conv_scaled(f, fft_size=None):
    n=f.size
    size = fft_size if fft_size is not None else next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    C=np.fft.irfft(F*F, size)
    return C[:2*n-1] / float(n)
```
For n=1? 2n-2=0, next_pow2(1)=1, C length1? f size1, returns C[:1]/1 okay.

`weighted_conv_no2`:
```
def weighted_conv_no2(f,w,n=None,fft_size=None):
    if n is None: n=f.size
    L=w.size
    size = fft_size if fft_size is not None else next_pow2(n+L-1)
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    Fr=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fr,size)
    return conv[n-1:n+n]
```
Need if n-1+n may exceed size? size>=n+L-1=3n-2, n-1+n=2n-1 size yes.

`softmax_weights`:
```
def softmax_weights(C, betaC):
    m=C.size
    if betaC <= 1e-12:
       return np.full(m,1.0/m)
    mx=C.max()
    sh=betaC*(C-mx)
    sh=np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0)
    E=np.exp(sh)
    s=E.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       idx=np.argsort(C)[::-1][:max(1,m//10)]
       w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1.0/idx.size; return w
    w=E/s
    return w
```
No eps uniform; maybe add small if beta high? Could add `eps=1e-5`: `w=(1-eps)*w+eps/m`. This smooths and prevents unique stale. But for max, maybe not. Use small eps=1e-4? Gradient average includes tiny all constraints. Let's choose `eps=1e-4`. For m=10k, uniform floor 1e-4/10000=1e-8 each, negligible. Good.

`apply_update`:
```
def apply_update(f, centered, eta, mode, n, fft_size, maxlog=6.0):
    if mode == 0:
       cat = -eta * centered
       cat = np.clip(cat, -maxlog, maxlog)
       nf = f * np.exp(cat)
    elif mode == 1:
       nf = f - eta * centered
       if np.any(nf < 0.0):
           nf = np.maximum(nf, 0.0)
    elif mode == 2:
       cat = -eta * f * centered
       cat = np.clip(cat, -maxlog, maxlog)
       nf = f * np.exp(cat)
    else: return None,None,float('inf')
    s=nf.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: return None,None,inf
    nf = nf * (float(n)/s)
    C = conv_scaled(nf, fft_size)
    sc = 2.0*float(C.max())
    return nf,C,sc
```
If mode1 creates all zeros after clip, skip. Maybe if eta large, many zeros; okay. To avoid zeros causing log issues in later multiplicative, `apply_update` leaves zeros; multiplicative mode 0 f*exp keeps zeros, so zero support may shrink. But zeros allowed. L-BFGS softmax can revive? Manual from zero cannot increase zeros (multiplicative). Euclidean mode can increase zeros. If many zeros, maybe okay. Could add floor after update: `nf = np.maximum(nf, 1e-12*n/len(nf))`? That may affect optimum but okay. To preserve, in apply_update: after normalize, set `nf[nf < 1e-15] = 0`? no. Let's floor tiny to e-18 to allow log in future? exp of zero is zero. If zeros, okay but non-smooth. Since parent likely positive, fine.

`smooth_noise`:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, max_modes=30, decay=6.0):
   N=next_pow2(n)
   F=np.zeros(N//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
   nm=min(max_modes, N//2)
   if nm <=0: return zeros
   k=np.arange(1,nm+1,dtype=float)
   amp=np.exp(-decay*k/max(1,nm))
   F[k]=(rng.normal(size=nm)+1j*rng.normal(size=nm))*amp
   vals=np.fft.irfft(F,N)[:n]
   vals-=vals.mean()
   sd=vals.std()
   if sd>0: vals/=sd
   return vals
```
F[k] where k int array; F indices integer. Need `F[k.astype(int)]`? `k` as int. Use `idx=np.arange(1,nm+1)`.

`random_template`:
```
def random_template(n, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    r=float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.35:
       f=1.0 + 0.6*smooth_noise(n,rng,40,4.0)
    elif r < 0.60:
       a=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)); b=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)); ph1=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); ph2=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       f=1.0 + a*np.sin(2*np.pi*x+ph1) + b*np.cos(2*np.pi*float(rng.integers(2,5))*x+ph2)
    elif r < 0.80:
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); sig=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)); f=np.exp(-((x-c)/(sig))**2) + 0.2
    else:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(-5,5)); beta=float(rng.uniform(0,10)); f=np.exp(alpha*x)*(1+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*float(rng.integers(1,4))*x))
    return norm_f(f)
```
Need ensure finite. norm_f clips finite.

`smooth_restart`:
```
def smooth_restart(f, best, rng, scale):
    n=f.size
    base = norm_f(0.5*f + 0.5*best) if best is not None else f
    noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, 40, 5.0)
    std = float(np.std(base))
    if std ==0: std=1.0
    pert = base + scale*std*noise
    return norm_f(pert)
```
Maybe also random template blend if scale large.

`interpolate_f`:
```
def interpolate_f(f, new_n):
    old=f.size
    p=f/f.sum()
    if old == new_n: return norm_f(f)
    x_o=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old,endpoint=False)
    x_n=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n,endpoint=False)
    pint=np.interp(x_n,x_o,p)
    return norm_f(pint*new_n)
```

`update_best` cannot be closure; we'll manually update in propose.

Manual search implementation:
```
def minimax_search(f0, time_limit, seed=0, beta0=1.0, beta1=100.0, steps=None, modes=None, verbose=True):
   t0=time.monotonic()
   f=norm_f(f0)
   n=f.size
   if n==0: return f,inf
   size=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   C=conv_scaled(f,size)
   cur=2.0*float(C.max())
   best_f=f.copy(); best_sc=cur
   if steps is None: steps=[0.03,0.1,0.4,1.2]
   if modes is None: modes=[0,1,2]
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+987)
   iter=0; stall=0
   print? controlled
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
      prog=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-t0)/max(time_limit,1e-9))
      beta=beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*(1.0-math.exp(-3.0*prog))
      if stall > 40: beta=min(500.0,beta*1.25)
      bf,bC,bc,beta_eta,mode = manual_step(...)
      if bc < cur - 1e-13:
         f=bf; C=bC; cur=bc
         if cur < best_sc:
             best_sc=cur; best_f=f.copy()
             if verbose: print(f"  minimax iter {iter} score {cur:.7f}")
         stall=0
      else:
         stall += 1
         if stall == 10 or stall % 50 == 0:
             scale=min(1.0,0.1+0.03*stall)
             f=smooth_restart(f,best_f,rng,scale)
             C=conv_scaled(f,size); cur=2*float(C.max())
             if cur < best_sc: update
         if stall > 300:
             f=parametric_blend(best_f,n,rng,0.4)
             C=conv_scaled(f,size); cur=...
             stall=100
      iter +=1
      if verbose and iter % 1000 ==0: print progress
   return best_f,best_sc
```
Need `parametric_blend` top-level:
```
def parametric_blend(best,n,rng,w):
    if best is None: return random_template(n,rng)
    t=random_template(n,rng)
    return norm_f((1-w)*best + w*t)
```

`softmax_f_from_u`:
```
def softmax_f_from_u(u,n):
    u=np.asarray(u,float)
    if u.size != n: n=u.size
    umax=u.max()
    e=np.exp(u-umax)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=1e-300:
       return np.ones(n)
    return e*(float(n)/s)
```
For u huge, e underflow for low; okay. For all -inf? not.

`init_u_from_f`:
```
def init_u_from_f(f):
   p=f/f.sum()
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-30)
   return np.log(p)
```
Since f sum n, p sum1. u values ~log(1/n) ± variation. Good.

`lbfgs_objective` stable logsumexp: `m=C.max()`, `sh=betaC*(C-m)`. If betaC large and C-m negative many, exp underflow; `Es` at least exp(0)=1 for max lag, so >1. Good. For betaC=0, use uniform, Csm=m? But we won't call beta=0 maybe. If betaC small, logsumexp/beta tends mean; okay.
```
def lbfgs_objective(u, n, betaC, fft_size, state=None):
    f=softmax_f_from_u(u,n)
    C=conv_scaled(f,fft_size)
    m=float(C.max())
    if betaC <= 1e-12:
        Csm = float(np.mean(C)) # or m?
        w = np.full(C.size,1.0/C.size)
    else:
        sh=betaC*(C-m); sh=np.clip(sh,-700,0); E=np.exp(sh); Es=E.sum()
        Csm = m + math.log(Es)/betaC
        w = E/Es
    J = float(n) * Csm
    h=weighted_conv_no2(f,w,n,fft_size)
    grad=2.0*h
    mean_g = float(np.dot(grad,f))/float(n)
    zgrad = f * (grad - mean_g)
    # maybe clip zgrad to avoid NaN
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(zgrad)): zgrad=np.zeros_like(u)
    return J, zgrad
```
Wait if betaC high, `Csm=m + log(Es)/beta`; stable. For uniform f, grad constant, zgrad zero. Good.

      state['best_score']=sc; state['best_f']=f.copy(); state['best_u']=x.copy()

      return False
   for _ in range()? no

Coordinate polish function:
```
def coordinate_polish(f0, time_limit, seed=123, K_active=120, betaC=20.0, verbose=False):
   t0=...
   f=norm_f; n; size=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   C=conv_scaled(f,size); cur=2*C.max(); best...
   rng=default_rng
   noimprove=0
   while time:
      # weights/h top? Use dense? For donor/receiver, compute h via dense with weights softmax betaC (C)
      w=softmax_weights(C,betaC)
      h=weighted_conv_no2(f,w,n,size)
      g=2*h - 2*h.mean()
      # donors top 10 positive, receivers bottom 10 negative
      order=np.argsort(g)
      donors=order[-10:][::-1]; receivers=order[:10]
      improved=False
      steps_frac=(0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5)
      for di in donors:
         for dst in receivers:
            if di==dst: continue
            fv=f[di]
            if fv<=0: continue
            for frac in steps_frac:
               amt=fv*frac
               nf=f.copy(); nf[di]-=amt; nf[dst]+=amt
               if nf[di]<0: continue
               s=nf.sum(); nf*=n/s
               Cn=conv_scaled(nf,size); scn=2*Cn.max()
               if scn < cur:
                   f=nf; C=Cn; cur=scn; improved=True
                   if cur<best... update
                   break
               # if tried larger frac maybe continue? if locking score, break? 
            if improved: break
         if improved: break
      if improved: noimprove=0
      else:
         noimprove+=1
         if noimprove>50:
            # random pair perturbation
            for _ in range(20):
               i=rng.integers(n); j=rng.integers(n); if i!=j: amt=f[i]*0.05; nf=...
               evaluate, accept if best? maybe just one.
            if noimprove>300: restart from best with noise
      # To avoid too many trials when no improve, break if noimprove>1000
   return best
```

```
def coordinate_polish(f0, time_limit, seed=321, betaC=20.0, verbose=True):
    t0=...
    f=norm_f(f0); n; size=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    C=conv_scaled(f,size); cur=2*C.max(); best...
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no=0; iter=0
    while time...:
       w=softmax_weights(C,betaC)
       h=weighted_conv_no2(f,w,n,size)
       g=2.0*h - 2.0*np.mean(h) # or h-mean
       if np.max(np.abs(g)) < 1e-14: break
       order=np.argsort(g)
       donors_order=order[-8:][::-1]
       rec_order=order[:8]
       improved=False
       fractions=(0.05,0.15,0.4)
       for di in donors_order:
          for dj in rec_order:
             if di==dj: continue
             vi=float(f[di])
             if vi <= 1e-15: continue
             for frac in fractions:
                 nf=f.copy()
                 amt=vi*frac
                 nf[di]-=amt; nf[dj]+=amt
                 if nf[di] < 0: nf[di]=0.0
                 s=nf.sum()
                 if s<=0: continue
                 nf*=float(n)/s
                 Cn=conv_scaled(nf,size); scn=2*float(Cn.max())
                 if scn < cur - 1e-13:
                     f=nf; C=Cn; cur=scn; improved=True
                     if cur<best_sc: update
                     break
                 if not improved and frac >=0.15 and scn > cur + 0.01: break # skip larger? optional
          if improved: break
       if improved: no=0
       else:
          no+=1
          if no % 50 == 0:
             f=smooth_restart(f,best_f,rng,min(0.5,0.1+0.02*no))
             C=conv_scaled(f,size); cur=...
             if cur<best_sc:
       if no>2000: break
       iter+=1; if verbose and iter%50==0: print
    return best_f,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

Approach
--------
1.  Normalize sequences to sum 1 (or scaled to length).
2.  Fast exact objective evaluation by FFT of the convolution square.
3.  Minimax soft-start gradient descent in several equivalent parameterizations:
       - Euclidean projected steps on the scaled density,
       - multiplicative log-density steps,
       - f-weighted multiplicative steps (same tangent direction as softmax L-BFGS).
4.  L-BFGS-B on a softmax log-parameterization with a smoothed min-plus objective.
5.  Coordinate-transfer polishing from active-lag gradients.
6.  Soft length interpolation and short re-searches.
The best sequence found under the time budget is returned.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    scipy_minimize = None

INF = float("inf")

def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def norm_f(arr):
    """Return a non-negative float array of same length normalized to sum = len(arr)."""
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a.astype(np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(n) / s)

def conv_scaled(f, fft_size=None):
    """
    Return C_k = (1/n) * sum_i f_i f_{k-i} for full linear convolution.
    If f has sum n, the evaluation score equals 2 * max(C).
    """
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = int(fft_size) if fft_size is not None else next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)
    return c[:2 * n - 1] / float(n)

def score_f(f):
    """Exact score for a non-negative sequence f (scale invariant)."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return INF
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return INF
    p = f / s
    C = conv_scaled(p)
    if C.size == 0:
        return INF
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(C))

def eval_list(seq):
    """Safe call to the provided global evaluate_sequence."""
    try:
        lst = [float(x) for x in seq]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return INF

def weighted_conv_no2(f, w, n=None, fft_size=None):
    """
    For w length 2n-1, return h_i = sum_k w_k f_{k-i} (valid indices).
    This is one half of the gradient of the smoothed objective.
    """
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if n is None:
        n = f.size
    L = w.size
    size = int(fft_size) if fft_size is not None else next_pow2(n + L - 1)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    Fr = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fr, size)
    return conv[n - 1:n + n]

def softmax_weights(C, betaC):
    """Probability weights over convolution lags, softmax(betaC * C)."""
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    m = C.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if betaC <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(C.max())
    sh = betaC * (C - mx)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0)
    E = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(E.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        # Very concentrated: fall back to top lags.
        q = max(1, min(m // 10, 64))
        idx = np.argpartition(C, -q)[-q:]
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = idx[idx >= 0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            w[0] = 1.0
        else:
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return w
    w = E / s
    # Tiny uniform floor avoids pathological one-constraint stuckness.
    eps = 1e-5
    if m > 1:
        w = (1.0 - eps) * w + (eps / float(m))
    return w

def apply_update(f, centered, eta, mode, n, fft_size, maxlog=6.0):
    """
    Candidate update in f (sum n) space.
    mode 0: f <- f * exp(-eta * centered), renormalize
    mode 1: f <- max(0, f - eta * centered), renormalize
    mode 2: f <- f * exp(-eta * f * centered), renormalize
    """
    if eta <= 0.0:
        return None, None, INF
    try:
        if mode == 0:
            cat = -eta * centered
            cat = np.clip(cat, -maxlog, maxlog)
            nf = f * np.exp(cat)
        elif mode == 1:
            nf = f - eta * centered
            if np.any(nf < 0.0):
                nf = np.maximum(nf, 0.0)
        elif mode == 2:
            cat = -eta * f * centered
            cat = np.clip(cat, -maxlog, maxlog)
            nf = f * np.exp(cat)
        else:
            return None, None, INF
    except Exception:
        return None, None, INF

    s = float(nf.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None, None, INF
    nf = nf * (float(n) / s)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(nf)):
        return None, None, INF
    C = conv_scaled(nf, fft_size)
    if C.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(C).all():
        return None, None, INF
    sc = float(2.0 * C.max())
    return nf, C, sc

def manual_step(f, C, betaC, n, fft_size, steps, modes, maxlog=6.0):
    """One soft-minimax step with exact line search over candidate steps."""
    w = softmax_weights(C, betaC)
    h = weighted_conv_no2(f, w, n, fft_size)
    grad = 2.0 * h

    # Tangent vectors.
    center_u = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    if n > 0:
        mean_w = float(np.dot(grad, f)) / float(n)
    else:
        mean_w = 0.0
    center_w = grad - mean_w

    best_f = f
    best_C = C
    best_sc = float(2.0 * C.max())
    best_eta = 0.0

    for mode in modes:
        center = center_w if mode == 2 else center_u
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(center)):
            break
        if float(np.max(np.abs(center))) <= 1e-15:
            continue
        for eta in steps:
            nf, nc, nsc = apply_update(f, center, float(eta), int(mode), n, fft_size, maxlog)
            if nf is None:
                continue
            if nsc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_f = nf
                best_C = nc
                best_sc = nsc
                best_eta = float(eta)
    return best_f, best_C, best_sc, best_eta

def smooth_noise(n, rng, max_modes=30, decay=5.0):
    """Zero-mean low-frequency smooth real noise, unit std."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = next_pow2(n)
    F = np.zeros(N // 2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
    nm = min(max(1, int(max_modes)), N // 2)
    if nm <= 0:
        vals = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        idx = np.arange(1, nm + 1, dtype=int)
        k = np.arange(1, nm + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        amp = np.exp(-float(decay) * k / float(nm))
        F[idx] = (rng.normal(size=nm) + 1j * rng.normal(size=nm)) * amp
        vals = np.fft.irfft(F, N)[:n]
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    vals -= vals.mean()
    sd = float(vals.std())
    if sd > 1e-15:
        vals /= sd
    else:
        vals = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return vals

def random_template(n, rng):
    """Generate a non-uniform non-negative template of length n, normalized to sum n."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.35:
        f = 1.0 + 0.6 * smooth_noise(n, rng, 35, 4.5)
    elif r < 0.60:
        a1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9))
        a2 = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.45))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        f1 = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        f2 = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        f = 1.0 + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * x + ph1) + a2 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f2 * x + ph2)
    elif r < 0.80:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        sig = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.45))
        f = np.exp(-((x - c) / sig) ** 2) + 0.25 * np.exp(-(x - (1.0 - c)) ** 2) * 4.0 - 0.8
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    else:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(-6.0, 6.0))
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        f = np.exp(alpha * x) * (1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * freq * x + phase))
    return norm_f(f)

def parametric_blend(best_f, n, rng, w):
    """Blend best sequence with a random template."""
    if best_f is None or best_f.size != n:
        return random_template(n, rng)
    t = random_template(n, rng)
    return norm_f((1.0 - w) * best_f + w * t)

def smooth_restart(f, best_f, rng, scale):
    """Perturb current sequence around best using smooth noise."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    if best_f is None or best_f.size != n:
        base = f
    else:
        base = norm_f(0.5 * f + 0.5 * best_f)
    noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, 35, 5.0)
    sd = float(np.std(base))
    if sd <= 1e-15:
        sd = 1.0
    pert = base + float(scale) * sd * noise
    return norm_f(pert)

def minimax_search(f0, time_limit, seed=0, beta0=1.0, beta1=100.0,
                   steps=None, modes=None, verbose=False):
    """Time-limited soft-minimax gradient search."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    f = norm_f(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return f, INF
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        C0 = conv_scaled(f)
        return f, float(2.0 * C0.max())

    fft_size = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    C = conv_scaled(f, fft_size)
    cur = float(2.0 * C.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_sc = cur

    if steps is None:
        steps = [0.05, 0.25, 1.0]
    if modes is None:
        modes = [0, 1, 2]
    steps = [float(x) for x in steps]
    modes = [int(x) for x in modes]

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 987654321)
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    last_print = 0
    print_initial = True

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        prog = min(1.0, elapsed / max(float(time_limit), 1e-9))
        beta = float(beta0) + (float(beta1) - float(beta0)) * (1.0 - math.exp(-3.0 * prog))
        if stall > 35:
            beta = min(500.0, beta * 1.25)

        bf, bc, bsc, _ = manual_step(f, C, beta, n, fft_size, steps, modes)

        if bsc < cur - 1e-13:
            f = bf
            C = bc
            cur = bsc
            stall = 0
            if cur < best_sc:
                best_sc = cur
                best_f = f.copy()
                if verbose and (it - last_print) >= 250:
                    last_print = it
                    print(f"    minimax improve: score={cur:.7f}, iter={it}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall == 8 or stall % 40 == 0:
                scale = min(0.85, 0.08 + 0.02 * min(stall, 30))
                nf = smooth_restart(f, best_f, rng, scale)
                nc = conv_scaled(nf, fft_size)
                nsc = float(2.0 * nc.max())
                if nsc > best_sc + 0.2:
                    nf = smooth_restart(best_f, best_f, rng, 0.04)
                    nc = conv_scaled(nf, fft_size)
                    nsc = float(2.0 * nc.max())
                f = nf
                C = nc
                cur = nsc
                if cur < best_sc:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best_f = f.copy()

            if stall > 260:
                nf = parametric_blend(best_f, n, rng, 0.35)
                nc = conv_scaled(nf, fft_size)
                nsc = float(2.0 * nc.max())
                if nsc > best_sc + 0.35:
                    nf = smooth_restart(best_f, best_f, rng, 0.15)
                    nc = conv_scaled(nf, fft_size)
                    nsc = float(2.0 * nc.max())
                f = nf
                C = nc
                cur = nsc
                stall = 80
                if cur < best_sc:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best_f = f.copy()

        it += 1
        if verbose and it - last_print >= 2000:
            last_print = it
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(f"    minimax: cur={cur:.7f}, best={best_sc:.7f}, t={elapsed:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return best_f, best_sc

def interpolate_f(f, new_n):
    """Interpolate a normalized density to another length and scale to sum new_n."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    old = f.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if old == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if old == new_n:
        return norm_f(f)
    if old == 1:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(f.sum())
    p = f / s if s > 1e-300 else np.full(old, 1.0 / old)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, endpoint=False)
    pint = np.interp(xn, xo, p)
    fnew = np.maximum(pint, 0.0) * float(new_n)
    return norm_f(fnew)

def coordinate_polish(f0, time_limit, seed=321, betaC=25.0, verbose=False):
    """Coordinate transfer polishing using active-lag gradient directions."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    f = norm_f(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        C0 = conv_scaled(f)
        return f, float(2.0 * C0.max())

    fft_size = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    C = conv_scaled(f, fft_size)
    cur = float(2.0 * C.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_sc = cur

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0

    fractions = (0.05, 0.15, 0.40)
    ndonor = 6
    nrecv = 6

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        w = softmax_weights(C, betaC)
        h = weighted_conv_no2(f, w, n, fft_size)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            break
        # Weighted tangent direction: high h increases smoothed max, move away.
        mean_h = float(np.dot(h, f)) / float(n)
        g = 2.0 * (h - mean_h)
        if float(np.max(np.abs(g))) <= 1e-15:
            break

        order = np.argsort(g)
        donors = order[-ndonor:][::-1]
        receivers = order[:nrecv]

        improved = False
        for di in donors:
            if improved:
                break
            di = int(di)
            vi = float(f[di])
            if vi <= 1e-15:
                continue
            for dj in receivers:
                if improved:
                    break
                dj = int(dj)
                if dj == di:
                    continue
                for frac in fractions:
                    nf = f.copy()
                    amt = vi * float(frac)
                    nf[di] -= amt
                    nf[dj] += amt
                    if nf[di] < 0.0:
                        nf[di] = 0.0
                    s = float(nf.sum())
                    if s <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    nf *= float(n) / s
                    Cn = conv_scaled(nf, fft_size)
                    scn = float(2.0 * Cn.max())
                    if scn < cur - 1e-13:
                        f = nf
                        C = Cn
                        cur = scn
                        improved = True
                        if cur < best_sc:
                            best_sc = cur
                            best_f = f.copy()
                        break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 80 == 0:
                scale = min(0.5, 0.05 + 0.02 * min(no_improve // 20, 20))
                f = smooth_restart(f, best_f, rng, scale)
                C = conv_scaled(f, fft_size)
                cur = float(2.0 * C.max())
                if cur < best_sc:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best_f = f.copy()
            if no_improve > 1800:
                break

        it += 1
        if verbose and it - last_print >= 100:
            last_print = it
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(f"    coordinate: cur={cur:.7f}, best={best_sc:.7f}, t={elapsed:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return best_f, best_sc

def softmax_f_from_u(u, n):
    """Return f = n * softmax(u), stable and non-negative."""
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    if u.size != n:
        n = u.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.clip(u, -50.0, 50.0)
    umax = float(u.max())
    e = np.exp(u - umax)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e * (float(n) / s)

def init_u_from_f(f):
    """Log likelihood start for softmax parameterization."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = f / s
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-30)
    return np.log(p)

def lbfgs_objective(u, n, betaC, fft_size, state=None):
    """
    Smoothed objective in log-softmax parameters:
        J = n * (1/beta) * logsumexp(beta * C)
    For large beta this approaches n * max(C), equivalent to the score.
    """
    try:
        if state is not None and time.monotonic() - state["t0"] > state["time_limit"] + 5.0:
            raise RuntimeError("lbfgs_timeout")
    except Exception:
        raise

    f = softmax_f_from_u(u, n)
    C = conv_scaled(f, fft_size)
    if C.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(C).all():
        return INF, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)

    mx = float(C.max())
    if betaC <= 1e-12:
        Csm = float(np.mean(C))
        w = np.full(C.size, 1.0 / C.size, dtype=np.float64)
        J = float(n) * Csm
    else:
        sh = betaC * (C - mx)
        sh = np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0)
        E = np.exp(sh)
        Es = float(E.sum())
        if Es <= 1e-300:
            Csm = mx
            w = np.zeros(C.size, dtype=np.float64)
            np.put(w, np.argmax(C), 1.0)
        else:
            Csm = mx + math.log(Es) / betaC
            w = E / Es
        J = float(n) * Csm

    h = weighted_conv_no2(f, w, n, fft_size)
    grad = 2.0 * h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(f, dtype=np.float64)
    mean_g = float(np.dot(grad, f)) / float(n)
    zgrad = f * (grad - mean_g)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(zgrad)):
        zgrad = np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(J), zgrad

def lbfgs_callback(x, state):
    """Callback for L-BFGS-B: track best exact score and stop on timeout."""
    try:
        n = int(x.size)
        f = softmax_f_from_u(x, n)
        C = conv_scaled(f, state["fft_size"])
        sc = float(2.0 * C.max())
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state["best_score"]:
            state["best_score"] = sc
            state["best_f"] = f.copy()
            state["best_u"] = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if time.monotonic() - state["t0"] > state["time_limit"] + 5.0:
            return False
    except Exception:
        pass
    return True

def lbfgs_smooth_search(f0, time_limit, betas=None, verbose=False):
    """L-BFGS-B search in log-softmax parameters with several tempering levels."""
    if scipy_minimize is None:
        f = norm_f(f0)
        return f, score_f(f)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    f = norm_f(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return f, INF
    fft_size = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    C = conv_scaled(f, fft_size)
    sc0 = float(2.0 * C.max())

    state = {
        "t0": t0,
        "time_limit": max(float(time_limit), 1.0),
        "best_score": sc0,
        "best_f": f.copy(),
        "best_u": init_u_from_f(f),
        "fft_size": fft_size,
    }

    if betas is None:
        betas = [1.0, 4.0, 12.0, 40.0, 90.0]

    bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * n

    if verbose:
        print(f"    L-BFGS start: n={n}, score={sc0:.7f}, budget={time_limit:.1f}s", flush=True)

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 >= state["time_limit"]:
            break
        x0 = state["best_u"].copy()
        maxiter = 160 if beta < 10.0 else 110
        try:
            res = scipy_minimize(
                lbfgs_objective,
                x0,
                args=(n, float(beta), fft_size, state),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                callback=lbfgs_callback,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                },
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None and np.isfinite(res.x).all():
                # Keep best_u from callback. Do not blindly move to final if it may be worse.
                pass
        except Exception as e:
            if verbose:
                print(f"    L-BFGS beta={beta} stopped: {e}", flush=True)
            if "timeout" in str(e).lower():
                break

    if verbose:
        print(f"    L-BFGS done: best={state['best_score']:.7f}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return state["best_f"], state["best_score"]

def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Main entry point. Returns a list of non-negative floats optimizing the
    evaluator before the time budget expires.
    """
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_time = min(float(budget_s) - 50.0, 1750.0)
    except Exception:
        total_time = 1750.0
    if total_time < 30.0:
        total_time = max(5.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)

    best_f = None
    best_sc = INF

    # Load the provided previous-best construction if available.
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
        pf = norm_f(parent)
        if pf.size > 0:
            pcs = score_f(pf)
            if np.isfinite(pcs):
                best_f = pf
                best_sc = pcs
                print(f"Parent loaded: n={pf.size}, score={best_sc:.7f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # If no parent exists, start from a strong random template at a reasonable length.
    if best_f is None or best_f.size == 0:
        n0 = 5200
        rng = np.random.default_rng(2024)
        best_temp = None
        best_temp_sc = INF
        for _ in range(6):
            t = random_template(n0, rng)
            s = score_f(t)
            if s < best_temp_sc:
                best_temp_sc = s
                best_temp = t
        if best_temp is not None:
            best_f = best_temp
            best_sc = best_temp_sc
        else:
            best_f = np.ones(5200, dtype=np.float64)
            best_sc = score_f(best_f)
        print(f"Generated initial: n={best_f.size}, score={best_sc:.7f}", flush=True)

    # Keep a search copy. Very long sequences can be interpolated for search speed,
    # while the original remains a fallback if it is better.
    if best_f is not None and best_f.size > 7000:
        search_f = interpolate_f(best_f, 5200)
    elif best_f.size < 400:
        search_f = interpolate_f(best_f, 5200)
    else:
        search_f = best_f.copy()

    def _try_update(f, sc, tag):
        nonlocal best_f, best_sc
        del tag  # tag not used, explicit local helper not needed by final top-level rule?
```
        if f is None or sc is INF:
            return
        f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
        if f.size == 0 or sc > best_sc or not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        best_f = f.copy()
        best_sc = float(sc)
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
        print(f"  *** NEW BEST: {best_sc:.7f} (n={best_f.size}, t={elapsed:.0f}s)", flush=True)

    # Fix: the above nested helper violates top-level restriction; replace with explicit code below.
    def _noop():
        pass

    # The final code below does not rely on the nested helpers.
    try:
        del _try_update
        del _noop
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Stage 1: main minimax search.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
    rem = total_time - elapsed
    t1 = min(rem * 0.48, 850.0)
    if t1 > 20.0:
        f1, s1 = minimax_search(search_f, t1, seed=11, beta0=1.0, beta1=90.0,
                                steps=[0.05, 0.25, 1.0], modes=[0, 1, 2], verbose=True)
        if s1 < best_sc:
            best_f = f1.copy()
            best_sc = float(s1)
            print(f"*** NEW BEST after minimax: {best_sc:.7f} (n={best_f.size})", flush=True)
        # Continue search on the best if length is valid.
        search_f = best_f.copy() if best_f.size > 0 else search_f

    # Stage 2: L-BFGS smooth polish if available.
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 80.0 and scipy_minimize is not None:
        t2 = min(rem * 0.28, 450.0)
        if t2 > 40.0:
            f2, s2 = lbfgs_smooth_search(search_f, t2, betas=[1.0, 4.0, 12.0, 40.0], verbose=True)
            if s2 < best_sc:
                best_f = f2.copy()
                best_sc = float(s2)
                print(f"*** NEW BEST after L-BFGS: {best_sc:.7f} (n={best_f.size})", flush=True)
            search_f = best_f.copy() if best_f.size > 0 else search_f
    else:
        if scipy_minimize is None:
            print("scipy unavailable; skipping L-BFGS stage", flush=True)

    # Stage 3: second minimax search with higher tempering focus.
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t3 = min(rem * 0.30, 450.0)
    if t3 > 30.0:
        f3, s3 = minimax_search(search_f, t3, seed=777, beta0=6.0, beta1=220.0,
                                steps=[0.03, 0.15, 0.6], modes=[0, 2, 1], verbose=True)
        if s3 < best_sc:
            best_f = f3.copy()
            best_sc = float(s3)
            print(f"*** NEW BEST after second minimax: {best_sc:.7f} (n={best_f.size})", flush=True)
        search_f = best_f.copy() if best_f.size > 0 else search_f

    # Stage 4: coordinate polish.
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t4 = min(rem * 0.30, 220.0)
    if t4 > 20.0:
        f4, s4 = coordinate_polish(search_f, t4, seed=31415, betaC=35.0, verbose=True)
        if s4 < best_sc:
            best_f = f4.copy()
            best_sc = float(s4)
            print(f"*** NEW BEST after coordinate polish: {best_sc:.7f} (n={best_f.size})", flush=True)
        search_f = best_f.copy() if best_f.size > 0 else search_f

    # Stage 5: nearby length exploration.
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 70.0 and best_f is not None and best_f.size > 0:
        n_base = int(best_f.size)
        candidates = []
        for off in (1, -1, 2, -2, 5, -5, 10, -10, 20, -20, 40, -40, 80, -80):
            nn = n_base + off
            if 500 <= nn <= 6500 and nn != n_base:
                if nn not in candidates:
                    candidates.append(nn)
        # Keep shortest list, but prefer small deviations.
        candidates.sort(key=lambda x: abs(x - n_base))

        for nn in candidates[:6]:
            rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            if rem < 45.0:
                break
            fi = interpolate_f(best_f, nn)
            sci = score_f(fi)
            if sci < best_sc:
                best_f = fi.copy()
                best_sc = float(sci)
                print(f"*** NEW BEST by interpolation: {best_sc:.7f} (n={nn})", flush=True)
            tlen = min(rem * 0.55, 70.0)
            if tlen > 10.0:
                fo, so = minimax_search(fi, tlen, seed=nn + 91, beta0=5.0, beta1=150.0,
                                        steps=[0.04, 0.18, 0.7], modes=[0, 2, 1], verbose=False)
                if so < best_sc:
                    best_f = fo.copy()
                    best_sc = float(so)
                    print(f"*** NEW BEST after length search: {best_sc:.7f} (n={nn})", flush=True)

    # Final validation and fallback.
    if best_f is None or best_f.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_f = np.ones(5200, dtype=np.float64)
        best_sc = score_f(best_f)

    # Normalize to sum 1 for output; non-negativity enforced.
    out = np.asarray(best_f, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        out = np.ones(5200, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out.sum())
    out = out / s

    # Verify with the official evaluator as a final sanity check.
    ev = eval_list(out)
    if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_sc:
        best_sc = ev

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
    print(f"FINAL: score={best_sc:.7f}, n={out.size}, time={elapsed:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
